+            workspace.data(), workspaceSize, info.data()));
+        const double* diagonalPointer = diagonal.data();
+        const double* scratchPointer = scratch.data();
+        double* scratchOutput = scratch.data();
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(leftPointers.data(), &diagonalPointer, sizeof(double*), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, gpu.stream));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(outputPointers.data(), &scratchOutput, sizeof(double*), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, gpu.stream));
+        const double one = 1.0, zero = 0.0;

+            CUBLAS_OP_T, CUBLAS_DIAG_NON_UNIT, b, b, &one, leftPointers.data(), b, outputPointers.data(), b, 1));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(leftPointers.data(), &scratchPointer, sizeof(double*), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, gpu.stream));

+            leftPointers.data(), b, rightPointers.data(), b, &zero, outputPointers.data(), b, 1));

+    void multiplyPanel(int k, int firstBlock, double* destination, double alpha, bool lookahead = false) {
+        if (grid.size == 1) gpu.synchronize();
+        if (lookahead) {
+            // Trailing tiles must have received the previous update before
+            // applying this one. The next panel and MPI exchange have already
+            // run concurrently with that previous update.
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(gpu.bulkDone));
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(gpu.panelsReady, gpu.stream));
+        }
+        active.clear(); bulkActive.clear();
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < tiles.size(); ++i)
+            if (tiles[i].column >= firstBlock) {
+                auto& indices = lookahead && tiles[i].column > firstBlock ? bulkActive : active;
+                indices.push_back(static_cast<int>(i));
+            }
+        launchBatch(active, k, destination, alpha, false);
+        if (lookahead) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamWaitEvent(gpu.bulkStream, gpu.panelsReady, 0));
+            launchBatch(bulkActive, k, destination, alpha, true);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(gpu.bulkDone, gpu.bulkStream));
+        }
+    }

 // glibc rand_r advances its 32-bit LCG three times per output. Jumping to
 // each row's initial state preserves the original seed-42 stream exactly,
 // independent of MPI ownership and the OpenMP schedule.
@@ -145,6 +180,22 @@

+std::vector<unsigned int> prepareRowSeeds(int n) {
+    if (compatibleRandomGenerator()) return {};
+    // Preserve rand_r semantics on other C libraries too. Only this less
+    // common initialization path needs a serial scan of the RNG states.
+    std::vector<unsigned int> seeds(n);
+    if (worldRank == 0) {
+        unsigned int seed = 42;
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            seeds[i] = seed;
+            for (int j = 0; j < n; ++j) rand_r(&seed);
+        }
+    }
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(seeds.data(), n, MPI_UNSIGNED, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    return seeds;
+}

+    // In row-major tile order, partial last-row tiles form a suffix, with
+    // the partial diagonal last. Three uniform batches avoid padded FLOPs.
+    void gemmBatches(cublasHandle_t handle, int inner, int leading, bool generating,
+                     const double* const* a, const double* const* bPointers,
+                     double* const* c, int count, const std::vector<int>* indices,
+                     double alpha, double beta) {
+        auto tileAt = [&](int i) -> const Tile& { return tiles[indices ? (*indices)[i] : i]; };
+        int full = count, partialDiagonal = 0;
+        const int edge = blocks ? width(blocks - 1) : b;
+        if (edge < b && count) {
+            full = 0;
+            while (full < count && tileAt(full).row != blocks - 1) ++full;
+            partialDiagonal = tileAt(count - 1).column == blocks - 1 ? 1 : 0;
+        }
+        const cublasOperation_t opA = generating ? CUBLAS_OP_T : CUBLAS_OP_N;
+        const cublasOperation_t opB = generating ? CUBLAS_OP_N : CUBLAS_OP_T;
+        auto launch = [&](int rows, int columns, int offset, int number) {
+            if (number)
+                BLAS_CHECK(cublasDgemmBatched(handle, opA, opB, rows, columns, inner, &alpha,
+                    a + offset, leading, bPointers + offset, leading, &beta, c + offset, b, number));
+        };
+        launch(b, b, 0, full);
+        launch(edge, b, full, count - full - partialDiagonal);
+        launch(edge, edge, count - partialDiagonal, partialDiagonal);
+    }

+    void launchBatch(const std::vector<int>& indices, int k, double* destination, double alpha, bool bulk) {
+        const int count = mpiCount(indices.size());
         if (!count) return;
+        auto& a = bulk ? bulkLeft : left;
+        auto& bPointers = bulk ? bulkRight : right;
+        auto& c = bulk ? bulkOutput : output;
+        auto* deviceA = bulk ? bulkLeftPointers.data() : leftPointers.data();
+        auto* deviceB = bulk ? bulkRightPointers.data() : rightPointers.data();
+        auto* deviceC = bulk ? bulkOutputPointers.data() : outputPointers.data();
+        const cudaStream_t stream = bulk ? gpu.bulkStream : gpu.stream;
         #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) if(count > 8)

-            const int index = active[i];
-            left[i] = rowPanel.data() + rowSlot[tiles[index].row] * elements;

+            const int index = indices[i];
+            a[i] = grid.size == 1 ? tile(tiles[index].row, k)
+                : rowData(k) + rowSlot[tiles[index].row] * elements;
+            bPointers[i] = grid.size == 1 ? tile(tiles[index].column, k)
+                : columnData(k) + columnSlot[tiles[index].column] * elements;
+            c[i] = destination + static_cast<size_t>(index) * elements;

-        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(rightPointers.data(), right.data(), count * sizeof(double*), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, gpu.stream));

+    }
+
+    void multiplyPanel(int k, int firstBlock, double* destination, double alpha, bool lookahead = false) {
+        if (grid.size == 1) gpu.synchronize();
+        if (lookahead) {
+            // Trailing tiles must have received the previous update before
+            // applying this one. The next panel and MPI exchange have already
+            // run concurrently with that previous update.
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(gpu.bulkDone));
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(gpu.panelsReady, gpu.stream));
+        }
+        active.clear(); bulkActive.clear();
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < tiles.size(); ++i)
+            if (tiles[i].column >= firstBlock) {
+                auto& indices = lookahead && tiles[i].column > firstBlock ? bulkActive : active;
+                indices.push_back(static_cast<int>(i));
+            }
+        launchBatch(active, k, destination, alpha, false);
+        if (lookahead) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamWaitEvent(gpu.bulkStream, gpu.panelsReady, 0));
+            launchBatch(bulkActive, k, destination, alpha, true);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(gpu.bulkDone, gpu.bulkStream));
+        }
     }

+    // This factors a separate identity tile and leaves the input untouched.
+    void prepareCholesky() {
+        if (!blocks) return;
+        DeviceBuffer<double> scratch(2 * elements);

+            workspace.data(), workspaceSize, info.data()));
+        const double* diagonalPointer = diagonal.data();
+        const double* scratchPointer = scratch.data();

+            CUBLAS_OP_T, CUBLAS_DIAG_NON_UNIT, width(blocks - 1), b, &one, diagonal.data(), b, scratch.data(), b));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(leftPointers.data(), &scratchPointer, sizeof(double*), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, gpu.stream));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(rightPointers.data(), &scratchPointer, sizeof(double*), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, gpu.stream));

+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(outputPointers.data(), &gemmOutput, sizeof(double*), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, gpu.stream));

+int solverWorkspace(Accelerator& gpu, int dimension, int leading, double* diagonal) {

 // glibc rand_r advances its 32-bit LCG three times per output. Jumping to
 // each row's initial state preserves the original seed-42 stream exactly,
 // independent of MPI ownership and the OpenMP schedule.
@@ -145,6 +180,22 @@
     return sizeof(unsigned int) == 4;

+std::vector<unsigned int> prepareRowSeeds(int n) {
+    if (compatibleRandomGenerator()) return {};
+    // Preserve rand_r semantics on other C libraries too. Only this less
+    // common initialization path needs a serial scan of the RNG states.
+    std::vector<unsigned int> seeds(n);
+    if (worldRank == 0) {
+        unsigned int seed = 42;
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            seeds[i] = seed;
+            for (int j = 0; j < n; ++j) rand_r(&seed);
+        }
+    }
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(seeds.data(), n, MPI_UNSIGNED, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    return seeds;
+}

+__global__ void identityDiagonal(double* matrix, int b) {

+    // In row-major tile order, partial last-row tiles form a suffix, with
+    // the partial diagonal last. Three uniform batches avoid padded FLOPs.
+    void gemmBatches(cublasHandle_t handle, int inner, int leading, bool generating,
+                     const double* const* a, const double* const* bPointers,
+                     double* const* c, int count, const std::vector<int>* indices,
+                     double alpha, double beta) {
+        auto tileAt = [&](int i) -> const Tile& { return tiles[indices ? (*indices)[i] : i]; };
+        int full = count, partialDiagonal = 0;
+        const int edge = blocks ? width(blocks - 1) : b;
+        if (edge < b && count) {
+            full = 0;
+            while (full < count && tileAt(full).row != blocks - 1) ++full;
+            partialDiagonal = tileAt(count - 1).column == blocks - 1 ? 1 : 0;
+        }
+        const cublasOperation_t opA = generating ? CUBLAS_OP_T : CUBLAS_OP_N;
+        const cublasOperation_t opB = generating ? CUBLAS_OP_N : CUBLAS_OP_T;
+        auto launch = [&](int rows, int columns, int offset, int number) {
+            if (number)
+                BLAS_CHECK(cublasDgemmBatched(handle, opA, opB, rows, columns, inner, &alpha,
+                    a + offset, leading, bPointers + offset, leading, &beta, c + offset, b, number));
+        };
+        launch(b, b, 0, full);
+        launch(edge, b, full, count - full - partialDiagonal);
+        launch(edge, edge, count - partialDiagonal, partialDiagonal);
+    }
+

+    void multiplyPanel(int k, int firstBlock, double* destination, double alpha, bool lookahead = false) {
+        if (grid.size == 1) gpu.synchronize();
+        if (lookahead) {
+            // Trailing tiles must have received the previous update before
+            // applying this one. The next panel and MPI exchange have already
+            // run concurrently with that previous update.
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(gpu.bulkDone));
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(gpu.panelsReady, gpu.stream));
+        }
+        active.clear(); bulkActive.clear();
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < tiles.size(); ++i)
+            if (tiles[i].column >= firstBlock) {
+                auto& indices = lookahead && tiles[i].column > firstBlock ? bulkActive : active;
+                indices.push_back(static_cast<int>(i));
+            }
+        launchBatch(active, k, destination, alpha, false);
+        if (lookahead) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamWaitEvent(gpu.bulkStream, gpu.panelsReady, 0));
+            launchBatch(bulkActive, k, destination, alpha, true);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(gpu.bulkDone, gpu.bulkStream));
+        }
     }

 // glibc rand_r advances its 32-bit LCG three times per output. Jumping to
 // each row's initial state preserves the original seed-42 stream exactly,
 // independent of MPI ownership and the OpenMP schedule.
@@ -145,6 +180,22 @@
     return sizeof(unsigned int) == 4;

+std::vector<unsigned int> prepareRowSeeds(int n) {
+    if (compatibleRandomGenerator()) return {};
+    // Preserve rand_r semantics on other C libraries too. Only this less
+    // common initialization path needs a serial scan of the RNG states.
+    std::vector<unsigned int> seeds(n);
+    if (worldRank == 0) {
+        unsigned int seed = 42;
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            seeds[i] = seed;
+            for (int j = 0; j < n; ++j) rand_r(&seed);
+        }
+    }
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(seeds.data(), n, MPI_UNSIGNED, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    return seeds;
+}

+    // In row-major tile order, partial last-row tiles form a suffix, with
+    // the partial diagonal last. Three uniform batches avoid padded FLOPs.
+    void gemmBatches(cublasHandle_t handle, int inner, int leading, bool generating,
+                     const double* const* a, const double* const* bPointers,
+                     double* const* c, int count, const std::vector<int>* indices,
+                     double alpha, double beta) {
+        auto tileAt = [&](int i) -> const Tile& { return tiles[indices ? (*indices)[i] : i]; };
+        int full = count, partialDiagonal = 0;
+        const int edge = blocks ? width(blocks - 1) : b;
+        if (edge < b && count) {
+            full = 0;
+            while (full < count && tileAt(full).row != blocks - 1) ++full;
+            partialDiagonal = tileAt(count - 1).column == blocks - 1 ? 1 : 0;
+        }
+        const cublasOperation_t opA = generating ? CUBLAS_OP_T : CUBLAS_OP_N;
+        const cublasOperation_t opB = generating ? CUBLAS_OP_N : CUBLAS_OP_T;
+        auto launch = [&](int rows, int columns, int offset, int number) {
+            if (number)
+                BLAS_CHECK(cublasDgemmBatched(handle, opA, opB, rows, columns, inner, &alpha,
+                    a + offset, leading, bPointers + offset, leading, &beta, c + offset, b, number));
+        };
+        launch(b, b, 0, full);
+        launch(edge, b, full, count - full - partialDiagonal);
+        launch(edge, edge, count - partialDiagonal, partialDiagonal);
+    }
+

+    void multiplyPanel(int k, int firstBlock, double* destination, double alpha, bool lookahead = false) {
+        if (grid.size == 1) gpu.synchronize();
+        if (lookahead) {
+            // Trailing tiles must have received the previous update before
+            // applying this one. The next panel and MPI exchange have already
+            // run concurrently with that previous update.
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(gpu.bulkDone));
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(gpu.panelsReady, gpu.stream));
+        }
+        active.clear(); bulkActive.clear();
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < tiles.size(); ++i)
+            if (tiles[i].column >= firstBlock) {
+                auto& indices = lookahead && tiles[i].column > firstBlock ? bulkActive : active;
+                indices.push_back(static_cast<int>(i));
+            }
+        launchBatch(active, k, destination, alpha, false);
+        if (lookahead) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamWaitEvent(gpu.bulkStream, gpu.panelsReady, 0));
+            launchBatch(bulkActive, k, destination, alpha, true);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(gpu.bulkDone, gpu.bulkStream));
+        }
     }

+int solverWorkspace(Accelerator& gpu, int dimension, int leading, double* diagonal) {
+    int count = 0;
+    SOLVER_CHECK(cusolverDnDpotrf_bufferSize(gpu.solver, CUBLAS_FILL_MODE_LOWER, dimension, diagonal, leading, &count));

 // glibc rand_r advances its 32-bit LCG three times per output. Jumping to
 // each row's initial state preserves the original seed-42 stream exactly,
 // independent of MPI ownership and the OpenMP schedule.

+std::vector<unsigned int> prepareRowSeeds(int n) {
+    int localCompatible = compatibleRandomGenerator() ? 1 : 0, allCompatible = 0;
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Allreduce(&localCompatible, &allCompatible, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MIN, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    if (allCompatible) return {};
+    // Preserve rand_r semantics on other C libraries too. Only this less
+    // common initialization path needs a serial scan of the RNG states.
+    std::vector<unsigned int> seeds(n);
+    if (worldRank == 0) {
+        unsigned int seed = 42;
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            seeds[i] = seed;

+    // In row-major tile order, partial last-row tiles form a suffix, with
+    // the partial diagonal last. Three uniform batches avoid padded FLOPs.
+    void gemmBatches(cublasHandle_t handle, int inner, int leading, bool generating,
+                     const double* const* a, const double* const* bPointers,
+                     double* const* c, int count, const std::vector<int>* indices,
+                     double alpha, double beta) {
+        auto tileAt = [&](int i) -> const Tile& { return tiles[indices ? (*indices)[i] : i]; };
+        int full = count, partialDiagonal = 0;
+        const int edge = blocks ? width(blocks - 1) : b;
+        if (edge < b && count) {
+            full = 0;
+            while (full < count && tileAt(full).row != blocks - 1) ++full;
+            partialDiagonal = tileAt(count - 1).column == blocks - 1 ? 1 : 0;
+        }
+        const cublasOperation_t opA = generating ? CUBLAS_OP_T : CUBLAS_OP_N;
+        const cublasOperation_t opB = generating ? CUBLAS_OP_N : CUBLAS_OP_T;
+        auto launch = [&](int rows, int columns, int offset, int number) {
+            if (number)
+                BLAS_CHECK(cublasDgemmBatched(handle, opA, opB, rows, columns, inner, &alpha,
+                    a + offset, leading, bPointers + offset, leading, &beta, c + offset, b, number));
+        };
+        launch(b, b, 0, full);
+        launch(edge, b, full, count - full - partialDiagonal);
+        launch(edge, edge, count - partialDiagonal, partialDiagonal);
+    }
+

+    void multiplyPanel(int k, int firstBlock, double* destination, double alpha, bool lookahead = false) {
+        if (grid.size == 1) gpu.synchronize();
+        if (lookahead) {
+            // Trailing tiles must have received the previous update before
+            // applying this one. The next panel and MPI exchange have already
+            // run concurrently with that previous update.
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(gpu.bulkDone));
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(gpu.panelsReady, gpu.stream));
+        }
+        active.clear(); bulkActive.clear();
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < tiles.size(); ++i)
+            if (tiles[i].column >= firstBlock) {
+                auto& indices = lookahead && tiles[i].column > firstBlock ? bulkActive : active;
+                indices.push_back(static_cast<int>(i));
+            }
+        launchBatch(active, k, destination, alpha, false);
+        if (lookahead) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamWaitEvent(gpu.bulkStream, gpu.panelsReady, 0));
+            launchBatch(bulkActive, k, destination, alpha, true);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(gpu.bulkDone, gpu.bulkStream));
+        }
+    }

+
 // glibc rand_r advances its 32-bit LCG three times per output. Jumping to
 // each row's initial state preserves the original seed-42 stream exactly,
 // independent of MPI ownership and the OpenMP schedule.
@@ -145,6 +180,24 @@
     return sizeof(unsigned int) == 4;

+std::vector<unsigned int> prepareRowSeeds(int n) {
+    int localCompatible = compatibleRandomGenerator() ? 1 : 0, allCompatible = 0;
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Allreduce(&localCompatible, &allCompatible, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MIN, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    if (allCompatible) return {};
+    // Preserve rand_r semantics on other C libraries too. Only this less
+    // common initialization path needs a serial scan of the RNG states.
+    std::vector<unsigned int> seeds(n);
+    if (worldRank == 0) {
+        unsigned int seed = 42;
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            seeds[i] = seed;
+            for (int j = 0; j < n; ++j) rand_r(&seed);
+        }
+    }
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(seeds.data(), n, MPI_UNSIGNED, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    return seeds;
+}

+    // In row-major tile order, partial last-row tiles form a suffix, with
+    // the partial diagonal last. Three uniform batches avoid padded FLOPs.
+    void gemmBatches(cublasHandle_t handle, int inner, int leading, bool generating,
+                     const double* const* a, const double* const* bPointers,
+                     double* const* c, int count, const std::vector<int>* indices,
+                     double alpha, double beta) {
+        auto tileAt = [&](int i) -> const Tile& { return tiles[indices ? (*indices)[i] : i]; };
+        int full = count, partialDiagonal = 0;
+        const int edge = blocks ? width(blocks - 1) : b;
+        if (edge < b && count) {
+            full = 0;
+            while (full < count && tileAt(full).row != blocks - 1) ++full;
+            partialDiagonal = tileAt(count - 1).column == blocks - 1 ? 1 : 0;
+        }
+        const cublasOperation_t opA = generating ? CUBLAS_OP_T : CUBLAS_OP_N;
+        const cublasOperation_t opB = generating ? CUBLAS_OP_N : CUBLAS_OP_T;
+        auto launch = [&](int rows, int columns, int offset, int number) {
+            if (number)
+                BLAS_CHECK(cublasDgemmBatched(handle, opA, opB, rows, columns, inner, &alpha,
+                    a + offset, leading, bPointers + offset, leading, &beta, c + offset, b, number));
+        };
+        launch(b, b, 0, full);
+        launch(edge, b, full, count - full - partialDiagonal);
+        launch(edge, edge, count - partialDiagonal, partialDiagonal);
+    }

+    void multiplyPanel(int k, int firstBlock, double* destination, double alpha, bool lookahead = false) {
+        if (grid.size == 1) gpu.synchronize();
+        if (lookahead) {
+            // Trailing tiles must have received the previous update before
+            // applying this one. The next panel and MPI exchange have already
+            // run concurrently with that previous update.
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(gpu.bulkDone));
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(gpu.panelsReady, gpu.stream));
+        }
+        active.clear(); bulkActive.clear();
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < tiles.size(); ++i)
+            if (tiles[i].column >= firstBlock) {
+                auto& indices = lookahead && tiles[i].column > firstBlock ? bulkActive : active;
+                indices.push_back(static_cast<int>(i));
+            }
+        launchBatch(active, k, destination, alpha, false);
+        if (lookahead) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamWaitEvent(gpu.bulkStream, gpu.panelsReady, 0));
+            launchBatch(bulkActive, k, destination, alpha, true);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(gpu.bulkDone, gpu.bulkStream));
+        }
+    }

+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(leftPointers.data(), &scratchPointer, sizeof(double*), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, gpu.stream));

+int solverWorkspace(Accelerator& gpu, int dimension, int leading, double* diagonal) {

 // glibc rand_r advances its 32-bit LCG three times per output. Jumping to
 // each row's initial state preserves the original seed-42 stream exactly,
 // independent of MPI ownership and the OpenMP schedule.
@@ -145,6 +180,24 @@
     return sizeof(unsigned int) == 4;

+std::vector<unsigned int> prepareRowSeeds(int n) {
+    int localCompatible = compatibleRandomGenerator() ? 1 : 0, allCompatible = 0;
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Allreduce(&localCompatible, &allCompatible, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MIN, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    if (allCompatible) return {};
+    // Preserve rand_r semantics on other C libraries too. Only this less
+    // common initialization path needs a serial scan of the RNG states.
+    std::vector<unsigned int> seeds(n);
+    if (worldRank == 0) {
+        unsigned int seed = 42;
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            seeds[i] = seed;
+            for (int j = 0; j < n; ++j) rand_r(&seed);
+        }
+    }
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(seeds.data(), n, MPI_UNSIGNED, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    return seeds;
+}

+    // In row-major tile order, partial last-row tiles form a suffix, with
+    // the partial diagonal last. Three uniform batches avoid padded FLOPs.
+    void gemmBatches(cublasHandle_t handle, int inner, int leading, bool generating,
+                     const double* const* a, const double* const* bPointers,
+                     double* const* c, int count, const std::vector<int>* indices,
+                     double alpha, double beta) {
+        auto tileAt = [&](int i) -> const Tile& { return tiles[indices ? (*indices)[i] : i]; };
+        int full = count, partialDiagonal = 0;
+        const int edge = blocks ? width(blocks - 1) : b;
+        if (edge < b && count) {
+            full = 0;
+            while (full < count && tileAt(full).row != blocks - 1) ++full;
+            partialDiagonal = tileAt(count - 1).column == blocks - 1 ? 1 : 0;
+        }
+        const cublasOperation_t opA = generating ? CUBLAS_OP_T : CUBLAS_OP_N;
+        const cublasOperation_t opB = generating ? CUBLAS_OP_N : CUBLAS_OP_T;
+        auto launch = [&](int rows, int columns, int offset, int number) {
+            if (number)
+                BLAS_CHECK(cublasDgemmBatched(handle, opA, opB, rows, columns, inner, &alpha,
+                    a + offset, leading, bPointers + offset, leading, &beta, c + offset, b, number));
+        };
+        launch(b, b, 0, full);
+        launch(edge, b, full, count - full - partialDiagonal);
+        launch(edge, edge, count - partialDiagonal, partialDiagonal);
+    }
+

+    void multiplyPanel(int k, int firstBlock, double* destination, double alpha, bool lookahead = false) {
+        if (grid.size == 1) gpu.synchronize();
+        if (lookahead) {
+            // Trailing tiles must have received the previous update before
+            // applying this one. The next panel and MPI exchange have already
+            // run concurrently with that previous update.
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(gpu.bulkDone));
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(gpu.panelsReady, gpu.stream));
+        }
+        active.clear(); bulkActive.clear();
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < tiles.size(); ++i)
+            if (tiles[i].column >= firstBlock) {
+                auto& indices = lookahead && tiles[i].column > firstBlock ? bulkActive : active;
+                indices.push_back(static_cast<int>(i));
+            }
+        launchBatch(active, k, destination, alpha, false);
+        if (lookahead) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamWaitEvent(gpu.bulkStream, gpu.panelsReady, 0));
+            launchBatch(bulkActive, k, destination, alpha, true);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(gpu.bulkDone, gpu.bulkStream));
+        }
+    }

import math, os, re, subprocess
binary = '/tmp/cholesky_hybrid_r2_default/cholesky'
original = '/tmp/cholesky_original_hybrid_r2'
env = dict(os.environ, OMP_NUM_THREADS='4')

def invoke(args, extra=None):
    run_env = dict(env)
    if extra:
        run_env.update(extra)
    return subprocess.run(args, env=run_env, text=True, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, timeout=90)

def statistics(output):
    stats = {}
    for line in output.splitlines():
        key, sep, value = line.partition(': ')
        if sep and (key in ('Sum', 'Min', 'Max') or key.startswith('Sample[')):
            stats[key] = float(value)
    return stats

for ranks, n in [(1, 0), (4, 1), (1, 37), (3, 257), (2, 513), (4, 777), (4, 1025)]:
    reference = invoke([original, '-n', str(n), '-v', '-r'])
    command = [binary, '-n', str(n), '-v', '-r']
    if ranks > 1:
        command = ['mpirun', '--allow-run-as-root', '--bind-to', 'none', '-np', str(ranks)] + command
    result = invoke(command)
    assert reference.returncode == 0, reference.stdout
    assert result.returncode == 0, result.stdout
    assert result.stdout.count('=== RESULTS ===') == 1, result.stdout
    assert result.stdout.count('Validation: PASSED') == 1, result.stdout
    assert f'Elements: {n*n}\\n' in result.stdout, result.stdout
    expected, actual = statistics(reference.stdout), statistics(result.stdout)
    assert expected.keys() == actual.keys(), result.stdout
    for key, value in expected.items():
        assert math.isclose(value, actual[key], rel_tol=1e-11, abs_tol=1e-11), (ranks, n, key, value, actual[key])
    print(f'PASS: n={n}, MPI ranks={ranks}, validation and original result statistics', flush=True)

for args in [['-n', '-1'], ['-n', 'bad'], ['-n'], ['--unknown']]:
    result = invoke([binary] + args, {'CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES': ''})
    assert result.returncode "'!= 0, result.stdout
help_result = invoke([binary, '"'-h'], {'CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES': ''})
assert help_result.returncode == 0 and 'Usage:' in help_result.stdout, help_result.stdout
required_cuda = invoke([binary, '-n', '4'], {'CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES': ''})
assert required_cuda.returncode "'!= 0 and '"'CUDA device is required' in required_cuda.stdout, required_cuda.stdout
print('PASS: CLI errors, help without a GPU, and mandatory CUDA execution', flush=True)

 // glibc rand_r advances its 32-bit LCG three times per output. Jumping to
 // each row's initial state preserves the original seed-42 stream exactly,
 // independent of MPI ownership and the OpenMP schedule.
@@ -145,6 +180,24 @@

+std::vector<unsigned int> prepareRowSeeds(int n) {
+    int localCompatible = compatibleRandomGenerator() ? 1 : 0, allCompatible = 0;
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Allreduce(&localCompatible, &allCompatible, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MIN, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    if (allCompatible) return {};
+    // Preserve rand_r semantics on other C libraries too. Only this less
+    // common initialization path needs a serial scan of the RNG states.
+    std::vector<unsigned int> seeds(n);
+    if (worldRank == 0) {
+        unsigned int seed = 42;
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            seeds[i] = seed;
+            for (int j = 0; j < n; ++j) rand_r(&seed);
+        }
+    }
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(seeds.data(), n, MPI_UNSIGNED, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    return seeds;
+}

+        auto launch = [&](int rows, int columns, int offset, int number) {
+            if (number)
+                BLAS_CHECK(cublasDgemmBatched(handle, opA, opB, rows, columns, inner, &alpha,
+                    a + offset, leading, bPointers + offset, leading, &beta, c + offset, b, number));
+        };
+        launch(b, b, 0, full);
+        launch(edge, b, full, count - full - partialDiagonal);
+        launch(edge, edge, count - partialDiagonal, partialDiagonal);
+    }
+
+    void launchBatch(const std::vector<int>& indices, int k, double* destination, double alpha, bool bulk) {
+        const int count = mpiCount(indices.size());
         if (!count) return;
+        auto& a = bulk ? bulkLeft : left;

+        auto& c = bulk ? bulkOutput : output;
+        auto* deviceA = bulk ? bulkLeftPointers.data() : leftPointers.data();
+        auto* deviceB = bulk ? bulkRightPointers.data() : rightPointers.data();
+        auto* deviceC = bulk ? bulkOutputPointers.data() : outputPointers.data();

+                : rowData(k) + rowSlot[tiles[index].row] * elements;
+            bPointers[i] = grid.size == 1 ? tile(tiles[index].column, k)

+    void multiplyPanel(int k, int firstBlock, double* destination, double alpha, bool lookahead = false) {
+        if (grid.size == 1) gpu.synchronize();
+        if (lookahead) {
+            // Trailing tiles must have received the previous update before
+            // applying this one. The next panel and MPI exchange have already
+            // run concurrently with that previous update.
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(gpu.bulkDone));
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(gpu.panelsReady, gpu.stream));
+        }
+        active.clear(); bulkActive.clear();
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < tiles.size(); ++i)
+            if (tiles[i].column >= firstBlock) {
+                auto& indices = lookahead && tiles[i].column > firstBlock ? bulkActive : active;
+                indices.push_back(static_cast<int>(i));
+            }
+        launchBatch(active, k, destination, alpha, false);
+        if (lookahead) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamWaitEvent(gpu.bulkStream, gpu.panelsReady, 0));
+            launchBatch(bulkActive, k, destination, alpha, true);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(gpu.bulkDone, gpu.bulkStream));
+        }
+    }

CMP0119
-------

.. versionadded:: 3.20

``LANGUAGE`` source file property explicitly compiles as specified
language.

The ``LANGUAGE`` source file property is documented to mean that the
source file is written in the specified language.  In CMake 3.19 and below,
setting this property causes CMake to compile the source file using the
compiler for the specified language.  However, it only passes an explicit
flag to tell the compiler to treat the source as the specified language
for MSVC-like, XL, and Embarcadero compilers for the ``CXX`` language.
CMake 3.20 and above prefer to also explicitly tell the compiler to use
the specified language using a flag such as ``-x c`` on all compilers
for which such flags are known.

This policy provides compatibility for projects that have not been updated
to expect this behavior.  For example, some projects were setting the
``LANGUAGE`` property to ``C`` on assembly-language ``.S`` source files
in order to compile them using the C compiler.  Such projects should be
updated to use ``enable_language(ASM)``, for which CMake will often choose
the C compiler as the assembler on relevant platforms anyway.

The ``OLD`` behavior for this policy is to interpret the ``LANGUAGE <LANG>``
property using its undocumented meaning to "use the ``<LANG>`` compiler".
The ``NEW`` behavior for this policy is to interpret the ``LANGUAGE <LANG>``
property using its documented meaning to "compile as a ``<LANG>`` source".

This policy was introduced in CMake version 3.20.
It may be set by ``cmake_policy()`` or ``cmake_minimum_required()``.
If it is not set, CMake does *not* warn, and uses ``OLD`` behavior.

.. note::
  The ``OLD`` behavior of a policy is
  ``deprecated by definition``
  and may be removed in a future version of CMake.

 // glibc rand_r advances its 32-bit LCG three times per output. Jumping to
 // each row's initial state preserves the original seed-42 stream exactly,
 // independent of MPI ownership and the OpenMP schedule.
@@ -145,6 +180,24 @@

+std::vector<unsigned int> prepareRowSeeds(int n) {
+    int localCompatible = compatibleRandomGenerator() ? 1 : 0, allCompatible = 0;
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Allreduce(&localCompatible, &allCompatible, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MIN, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    if (allCompatible) return {};
+    // Preserve rand_r semantics on other C libraries too. Only this less
+    // common initialization path needs a serial scan of the RNG states.
+    std::vector<unsigned int> seeds(n);
+    if (worldRank == 0) {
+        unsigned int seed = 42;
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            seeds[i] = seed;
+            for (int j = 0; j < n; ++j) rand_r(&seed);
+        }
+    }
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(seeds.data(), n, MPI_UNSIGNED, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    return seeds;
+}

+    // In row-major tile order, partial last-row tiles form a suffix, with
+    // the partial diagonal last. Three uniform batches avoid padded FLOPs.
+    void gemmBatches(cublasHandle_t handle, int inner, int leading, bool generating,
+                     const double* const* a, const double* const* bPointers,
+                     double* const* c, int count, const std::vector<int>* indices,
+                     double alpha, double beta) {
+        auto tileAt = [&](int i) -> const Tile& { return tiles[indices ? (*indices)[i] : i]; };
+        int full = count, partialDiagonal = 0;
+        const int edge = blocks ? width(blocks - 1) : b;
+        if (edge < b && count) {
+            full = 0;
+            while (full < count && tileAt(full).row != blocks - 1) ++full;
+            partialDiagonal = tileAt(count - 1).column == blocks - 1 ? 1 : 0;
+        }
+        const cublasOperation_t opA = generating ? CUBLAS_OP_T : CUBLAS_OP_N;
+        const cublasOperation_t opB = generating ? CUBLAS_OP_N : CUBLAS_OP_T;
+        auto launch = [&](int rows, int columns, int offset, int number) {
+            if (number)
+                BLAS_CHECK(cublasDgemmBatched(handle, opA, opB, rows, columns, inner, &alpha,
+                    a + offset, leading, bPointers + offset, leading, &beta, c + offset, b, number));
+        };
+        launch(b, b, 0, full);
+        launch(edge, b, full, count - full - partialDiagonal);
+        launch(edge, edge, count - partialDiagonal, partialDiagonal);
+    }
+
+    void launchBatch(const std::vector<int>& indices, int k, double* destination, double alpha, bool bulk) {
+        const int count = mpiCount(indices.size());
         if (!count) return;
+        auto& a = bulk ? bulkLeft : left;
+        auto& bPointers = bulk ? bulkRight : right;
+        auto& c = bulk ? bulkOutput : output;
+        auto* deviceA = bulk ? bulkLeftPointers.data() : leftPointers.data();
+        auto* deviceB = bulk ? bulkRightPointers.data() : rightPointers.data();
+        auto* deviceC = bulk ? bulkOutputPointers.data() : outputPointers.data();
+        const cudaStream_t stream = bulk ? gpu.bulkStream : gpu.stream;
         #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) if(count > 8)
         for (int i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
-            const int index = active[i];
-            left[i] = rowPanel.data() + rowSlot[tiles[index].row] * elements;
-            right[i] = columnPanel.data() + columnSlot[tiles[index].column] * elements;
-            output[i] = destination + static_cast<size_t>(index) * elements;
+            const int index = indices[i];
+            a[i] = grid.size == 1 ? tile(tiles[index].row, k)
+                : rowData(k) + rowSlot[tiles[index].row] * elements;
+            bPointers[i] = grid.size == 1 ? tile(tiles[index].column, k)
+                : columnData(k) + columnSlot[tiles[index].column] * elements;
+            c[i] = destination + static_cast<size_t>(index) * elements;
         }
-        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(leftPointers.data(), left.data(), count * sizeof(double*), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, gpu.stream));
-        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(rightPointers.data(), right.data(), count * sizeof(double*), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, gpu.stream));
-        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(outputPointers.data(), output.data(), count * sizeof(double*), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, gpu.stream));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(deviceA, a.data(), count * sizeof(double*), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(deviceB, bPointers.data(), count * sizeof(double*), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(deviceC, c.data(), count * sizeof(double*), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));
         const double beta = 1.0;
-        BLAS_CHECK(cublasDgemmBatched(gpu.blas, CUBLAS_OP_N, CUBLAS_OP_T, b, b, b, &alpha,
-            leftPointers.data(), b, rightPointers.data(), b, &beta, outputPointers.data(), b, count));
+        gemmBatches(bulk ? gpu.bulkBlas : gpu.blas, width(k), b, false,
+            deviceA, deviceB, deviceC, count, &indices, alpha, beta);
     }
 
+    void multiplyPanel(int k, int firstBlock, double* destination, double alpha, bool lookahead = false) {
+        if (grid.size == 1) gpu.synchronize();
+        if (lookahead) {
+            // Trailing tiles must have received the previous update before
+            // applying this one. The next panel and MPI exchange have already
+            // run concurrently with that previous update.
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(gpu.bulkDone));
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(gpu.panelsReady, gpu.stream));
+        }
+        active.clear(); bulkActive.clear();
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < tiles.size(); ++i)
+            if (tiles[i].column >= firstBlock) {
+                auto& indices = lookahead && tiles[i].column > firstBlock ? bulkActive : active;
+                indices.push_back(static_cast<int>(i));
+            }
+        launchBatch(active, k, destination, alpha, false);
+        if (lookahead) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamWaitEvent(gpu.bulkStream, gpu.panelsReady, 0));
+            launchBatch(bulkActive, k, destination, alpha, true);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(gpu.bulkDone, gpu.bulkStream));
+        }
+    }

+int solverWorkspace(Accelerator& gpu, int dimension, int leading, double* diagonal) {

 // glibc rand_r advances its 32-bit LCG three times per output. Jumping to
 // each row's initial state preserves the original seed-42 stream exactly,
 // independent of MPI ownership and the OpenMP schedule.
@@ -145,6 +180,24 @@
     return sizeof(unsigned int) == 4;

+std::vector<unsigned int> prepareRowSeeds(int n) {
+    int localCompatible = compatibleRandomGenerator() ? 1 : 0, allCompatible = 0;
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Allreduce(&localCompatible, &allCompatible, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MIN, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    if (allCompatible) return {};
+    // Preserve rand_r semantics on other C libraries too. Only this less
+    // common initialization path needs a serial scan of the RNG states.
+    std::vector<unsigned int> seeds(n);
+    if (worldRank == 0) {
+        unsigned int seed = 42;
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            seeds[i] = seed;
+            for (int j = 0; j < n; ++j) rand_r(&seed);
+        }
+    }
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(seeds.data(), n, MPI_UNSIGNED, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    return seeds;
+}

+    // In row-major tile order, partial last-row tiles form a suffix, with
+    // the partial diagonal last. Three uniform batches avoid padded FLOPs.
+    void gemmBatches(cublasHandle_t handle, int inner, int leading, bool generating,
+                     const double* const* a, const double* const* bPointers,
+                     double* const* c, int count, const std::vector<int>* indices,
+                     double alpha, double beta) {
+        auto tileAt = [&](int i) -> const Tile& { return tiles[indices ? (*indices)[i] : i]; };
+        int full = count, partialDiagonal = 0;
+        const int edge = blocks ? width(blocks - 1) : b;
+        if (edge < b && count) {
+            full = 0;
+            while (full < count && tileAt(full).row != blocks - 1) ++full;
+            partialDiagonal = tileAt(count - 1).column == blocks - 1 ? 1 : 0;
+        }
+        const cublasOperation_t opA = generating ? CUBLAS_OP_T : CUBLAS_OP_N;
+        const cublasOperation_t opB = generating ? CUBLAS_OP_N : CUBLAS_OP_T;
+        auto launch = [&](int rows, int columns, int offset, int number) {
+            if (number)
+                BLAS_CHECK(cublasDgemmBatched(handle, opA, opB, rows, columns, inner, &alpha,
+                    a + offset, leading, bPointers + offset, leading, &beta, c + offset, b, number));
+        };
+        launch(b, b, 0, full);
+        launch(edge, b, full, count - full - partialDiagonal);
+        launch(edge, edge, count - partialDiagonal, partialDiagonal);
+    }
+

+    void multiplyPanel(int k, int firstBlock, double* destination, double alpha, bool lookahead = false) {
+        if (grid.size == 1) gpu.synchronize();
+        if (lookahead) {
+            // Trailing tiles must have received the previous update before
+            // applying this one. The next panel and MPI exchange have already
+            // run concurrently with that previous update.
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(gpu.bulkDone));
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(gpu.panelsReady, gpu.stream));
+        }
+        active.clear(); bulkActive.clear();
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < tiles.size(); ++i)
+            if (tiles[i].column >= firstBlock) {
+                auto& indices = lookahead && tiles[i].column > firstBlock ? bulkActive : active;
+                indices.push_back(static_cast<int>(i));
+            }
+        launchBatch(active, k, destination, alpha, false);
+        if (lookahead) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamWaitEvent(gpu.bulkStream, gpu.panelsReady, 0));
+            launchBatch(bulkActive, k, destination, alpha, true);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(gpu.bulkDone, gpu.bulkStream));
+        }
     }

 // glibc rand_r advances its 32-bit LCG three times per output. Jumping to
 // each row's initial state preserves the original seed-42 stream exactly,
 // independent of MPI ownership and the OpenMP schedule.
@@ -145,6 +180,24 @@

+std::vector<unsigned int> prepareRowSeeds(int n) {
+    int localCompatible = compatibleRandomGenerator() ? 1 : 0, allCompatible = 0;
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Allreduce(&localCompatible, &allCompatible, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MIN, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    if (allCompatible) return {};
+    // Preserve rand_r semantics on other C libraries too. Only this less
+    // common initialization path needs a serial scan of the RNG states.
+    std::vector<unsigned int> seeds(n);
+    if (worldRank == 0) {
+        unsigned int seed = 42;
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            seeds[i] = seed;
+            for (int j = 0; j < n; ++j) rand_r(&seed);
+        }
+    }
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(seeds.data(), n, MPI_UNSIGNED, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    return seeds;
+}

-    void multiplyPanel(int firstBlock, double* destination, double alpha) {

+    // In row-major tile order, partial last-row tiles form a suffix, with
+    // the partial diagonal last. Three uniform batches avoid padded FLOPs.
+    void gemmBatches(cublasHandle_t handle, int inner, int leading, bool generating,
+                     const double* const* a, const double* const* bPointers,
+                     double* const* c, int count, const std::vector<int>* indices,
+                     double alpha, double beta) {
+        auto tileAt = [&](int i) -> const Tile& { return tiles[indices ? (*indices)[i] : i]; };
+        int full = count, partialDiagonal = 0;
+        const int edge = blocks ? width(blocks - 1) : b;
+        if (edge < b && count) {
+            full = 0;
+            while (full < count && tileAt(full).row != blocks - 1) ++full;
+            partialDiagonal = tileAt(count - 1).column == blocks - 1 ? 1 : 0;
+        }
+        const cublasOperation_t opA = generating ? CUBLAS_OP_T : CUBLAS_OP_N;
+        const cublasOperation_t opB = generating ? CUBLAS_OP_N : CUBLAS_OP_T;
+        auto launch = [&](int rows, int columns, int offset, int number) {
+            if (number)
+                BLAS_CHECK(cublasDgemmBatched(handle, opA, opB, rows, columns, inner, &alpha,
+                    a + offset, leading, bPointers + offset, leading, &beta, c + offset, b, number));
+        };
+        launch(b, b, 0, full);
+        launch(edge, b, full, count - full - partialDiagonal);
+        launch(edge, edge, count - partialDiagonal, partialDiagonal);
+    }
+
+    void launchBatch(const std::vector<int>& indices, int k, double* destination, double alpha, bool bulk) {
+        const int count = mpiCount(indices.size());
         if (!count) return;
+        auto& a = bulk ? bulkLeft : left;
+        auto& bPointers = bulk ? bulkRight : right;
+        auto& c = bulk ? bulkOutput : output;
+        auto* deviceA = bulk ? bulkLeftPointers.data() : leftPointers.data();
+        auto* deviceB = bulk ? bulkRightPointers.data() : rightPointers.data();
+        auto* deviceC = bulk ? bulkOutputPointers.data() : outputPointers.data();
+        const cudaStream_t stream = bulk ? gpu.bulkStream : gpu.stream;
         #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) if(count > 8)
         for (int i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
-            const int index = active[i];
-            left[i] = rowPanel.data() + rowSlot[tiles[index].row] * elements;
-            right[i] = columnPanel.data() + columnSlot[tiles[index].column] * elements;
-            output[i] = destination + static_cast<size_t>(index) * elements;
+            const int index = indices[i];
+            a[i] = grid.size == 1 ? tile(tiles[index].row, k)
+                : rowData(k) + rowSlot[tiles[index].row] * elements;
+            bPointers[i] = grid.size == 1 ? tile(tiles[index].column, k)
+                : columnData(k) + columnSlot[tiles[index].column] * elements;
+            c[i] = destination + static_cast<size_t>(index) * elements;
         }
-        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(leftPointers.data(), left.data(), count * sizeof(double*), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, gpu.stream));
-        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(rightPointers.data(), right.data(), count * sizeof(double*), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, gpu.stream));
-        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(outputPointers.data(), output.data(), count * sizeof(double*), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, gpu.stream));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(deviceA, a.data(), count * sizeof(double*), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(deviceB, bPointers.data(), count * sizeof(double*), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(deviceC, c.data(), count * sizeof(double*), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));
         const double beta = 1.0;
-        BLAS_CHECK(cublasDgemmBatched(gpu.blas, CUBLAS_OP_N, CUBLAS_OP_T, b, b, b, &alpha,
-            leftPointers.data(), b, rightPointers.data(), b, &beta, outputPointers.data(), b, count));
+        gemmBatches(bulk ? gpu.bulkBlas : gpu.blas, width(k), b, false,
+            deviceA, deviceB, deviceC, count, &indices, alpha, beta);
+    }
+
+    void multiplyPanel(int k, int firstBlock, double* destination, double alpha, bool lookahead = false) {
+        if (grid.size == 1) gpu.synchronize();
+        if (lookahead) {
+            // Trailing tiles must have received the previous update before
+            // applying this one. The next panel and MPI exchange have already
+            // run concurrently with that previous update.
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(gpu.bulkDone));
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(gpu.panelsReady, gpu.stream));
+        }
+        active.clear(); bulkActive.clear();
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < tiles.size(); ++i)
+            if (tiles[i].column >= firstBlock) {
+                auto& indices = lookahead && tiles[i].column > firstBlock ? bulkActive : active;
+                indices.push_back(static_cast<int>(i));
+            }
+        launchBatch(active, k, destination, alpha, false);
+        if (lookahead) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamWaitEvent(gpu.bulkStream, gpu.panelsReady, 0));
+            launchBatch(bulkActive, k, destination, alpha, true);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(gpu.bulkDone, gpu.bulkStream));
+        }
     }

+            workspace.data(), workspaceSize, info.data()));
+        const double* diagonalPointer = diagonal.data();
+        const double* scratchPointer = scratch.data();
+        double* scratchOutput = scratch.data();
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(leftPointers.data(), &diagonalPointer, sizeof(double*), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, gpu.stream));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(outputPointers.data(), &scratchOutput, sizeof(double*), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, gpu.stream));
+        const double one = 1.0, zero = 0.0;

+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(leftPointers.data(), &scratchPointer, sizeof(double*), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, gpu.stream));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(rightPointers.data(), &scratchPointer, sizeof(double*), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, gpu.stream));

 // glibc rand_r advances its 32-bit LCG three times per output. Jumping to
 // each row's initial state preserves the original seed-42 stream exactly,
 // independent of MPI ownership and the OpenMP schedule.
@@ -145,6 +180,24 @@
     return sizeof(unsigned int) == 4;

+std::vector<unsigned int> prepareRowSeeds(int n) {
+    int localCompatible = compatibleRandomGenerator() ? 1 : 0, allCompatible = 0;
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Allreduce(&localCompatible, &allCompatible, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MIN, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    if (allCompatible) return {};
+    // Preserve rand_r semantics on other C libraries too. Only this less
+    // common initialization path needs a serial scan of the RNG states.
+    std::vector<unsigned int> seeds(n);
+    if (worldRank == 0) {
+        unsigned int seed = 42;
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            seeds[i] = seed;
+            for (int j = 0; j < n; ++j) rand_r(&seed);
+        }
+    }
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(seeds.data(), n, MPI_UNSIGNED, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    return seeds;
+}

+    // In row-major tile order, partial last-row tiles form a suffix, with
+    // the partial diagonal last. Three uniform batches avoid padded FLOPs.
+    void gemmBatches(cublasHandle_t handle, int inner, int leading, bool generating,
+                     const double* const* a, const double* const* bPointers,
+                     double* const* c, int count, const std::vector<int>* indices,
+                     double alpha, double beta) {
+        auto tileAt = [&](int i) -> const Tile& { return tiles[indices ? (*indices)[i] : i]; };
+        int full = count, partialDiagonal = 0;
+        const int edge = blocks ? width(blocks - 1) : b;
+        if (edge < b && count) {
+            full = 0;
+            while (full < count && tileAt(full).row != blocks - 1) ++full;
+            partialDiagonal = tileAt(count - 1).column == blocks - 1 ? 1 : 0;
+        }
+        const cublasOperation_t opA = generating ? CUBLAS_OP_T : CUBLAS_OP_N;
+        const cublasOperation_t opB = generating ? CUBLAS_OP_N : CUBLAS_OP_T;
+        auto launch = [&](int rows, int columns, int offset, int number) {
+            if (number)
+                BLAS_CHECK(cublasDgemmBatched(handle, opA, opB, rows, columns, inner, &alpha,
+                    a + offset, leading, bPointers + offset, leading, &beta, c + offset, b, number));
+        };
+        launch(b, b, 0, full);
+        launch(edge, b, full, count - full - partialDiagonal);
+        launch(edge, edge, count - partialDiagonal, partialDiagonal);
+    }
+

+    void multiplyPanel(int k, int firstBlock, double* destination, double alpha, bool lookahead = false) {
+        if (grid.size == 1) gpu.synchronize();
+        if (lookahead) {
+            // Trailing tiles must have received the previous update before
+            // applying this one. The next panel and MPI exchange have already
+            // run concurrently with that previous update.
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(gpu.bulkDone));
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(gpu.panelsReady, gpu.stream));
+        }
+        active.clear(); bulkActive.clear();
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < tiles.size(); ++i)
+            if (tiles[i].column >= firstBlock) {
+                auto& indices = lookahead && tiles[i].column > firstBlock ? bulkActive : active;
+                indices.push_back(static_cast<int>(i));
+            }
+        launchBatch(active, k, destination, alpha, false);
+        if (lookahead) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamWaitEvent(gpu.bulkStream, gpu.panelsReady, 0));
+            launchBatch(bulkActive, k, destination, alpha, true);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(gpu.bulkDone, gpu.bulkStream));
+        }
     }

+int solverWorkspace(Accelerator& gpu, int dimension, int leading, double* diagonal) {
+    int count = 0;
+    SOLVER_CHECK(cusolverDnDpotrf_bufferSize(gpu.solver, CUBLAS_FILL_MODE_LOWER, dimension, diagonal, leading, &count));

 // glibc rand_r advances its 32-bit LCG three times per output. Jumping to
 // each row's initial state preserves the original seed-42 stream exactly,
 // independent of MPI ownership and the OpenMP schedule.
@@ -145,6 +180,24 @@
     return sizeof(unsigned int) == 4;

+std::vector<unsigned int> prepareRowSeeds(int n) {
+    int localCompatible = compatibleRandomGenerator() ? 1 : 0, allCompatible = 0;
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Allreduce(&localCompatible, &allCompatible, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MIN, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    if (allCompatible) return {};
+    // Preserve rand_r semantics on other C libraries too. Only this less
+    // common initialization path needs a serial scan of the RNG states.
+    std::vector<unsigned int> seeds(n);
+    if (worldRank == 0) {
+        unsigned int seed = 42;
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            seeds[i] = seed;
+            for (int j = 0; j < n; ++j) rand_r(&seed);
+        }
+    }
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(seeds.data(), n, MPI_UNSIGNED, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    return seeds;
+}

+    // In row-major tile order, partial last-row tiles form a suffix, with
+    // the partial diagonal last. Three uniform batches avoid padded FLOPs.
+    void gemmBatches(cublasHandle_t handle, int inner, int leading, bool generating,
+                     const double* const* a, const double* const* bPointers,
+                     double* const* c, int count, const std::vector<int>* indices,
+                     double alpha, double beta) {
+        auto tileAt = [&](int i) -> const Tile& { return tiles[indices ? (*indices)[i] : i]; };
+        int full = count, partialDiagonal = 0;
+        const int edge = blocks ? width(blocks - 1) : b;
+        if (edge < b && count) {
+            full = 0;
+            while (full < count && tileAt(full).row != blocks - 1) ++full;
+            partialDiagonal = tileAt(count - 1).column == blocks - 1 ? 1 : 0;
+        }
+        const cublasOperation_t opA = generating ? CUBLAS_OP_T : CUBLAS_OP_N;
+        const cublasOperation_t opB = generating ? CUBLAS_OP_N : CUBLAS_OP_T;
+        auto launch = [&](int rows, int columns, int offset, int number) {
+            if (number)
+                BLAS_CHECK(cublasDgemmBatched(handle, opA, opB, rows, columns, inner, &alpha,
+                    a + offset, leading, bPointers + offset, leading, &beta, c + offset, b, number));
+        };
+        launch(b, b, 0, full);
+        launch(edge, b, full, count - full - partialDiagonal);
+        launch(edge, edge, count - partialDiagonal, partialDiagonal);
+    }
+

+    void multiplyPanel(int k, int firstBlock, double* destination, double alpha, bool lookahead = false) {
+        if (grid.size == 1) gpu.synchronize();
+        if (lookahead) {
+            // Trailing tiles must have received the previous update before
+            // applying this one. The next panel and MPI exchange have already
+            // run concurrently with that previous update.
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(gpu.bulkDone));
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(gpu.panelsReady, gpu.stream));
+        }
+        active.clear(); bulkActive.clear();
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < tiles.size(); ++i)
+            if (tiles[i].column >= firstBlock) {
+                auto& indices = lookahead && tiles[i].column > firstBlock ? bulkActive : active;
+                indices.push_back(static_cast<int>(i));
+            }
+        launchBatch(active, k, destination, alpha, false);
+        if (lookahead) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamWaitEvent(gpu.bulkStream, gpu.panelsReady, 0));
+            launchBatch(bulkActive, k, destination, alpha, true);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(gpu.bulkDone, gpu.bulkStream));
+        }
     }

 // glibc rand_r advances its 32-bit LCG three times per output. Jumping to
 // each row's initial state preserves the original seed-42 stream exactly,
 // independent of MPI ownership and the OpenMP schedule.
@@ -145,6 +180,24 @@

+std::vector<unsigned int> prepareRowSeeds(int n) {
+    int localCompatible = compatibleRandomGenerator() ? 1 : 0, allCompatible = 0;
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Allreduce(&localCompatible, &allCompatible, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MIN, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    if (allCompatible) return {};
+    // Preserve rand_r semantics on other C libraries too. Only this less
+    // common initialization path needs a serial scan of the RNG states.
+    std::vector<unsigned int> seeds(n);
+    if (worldRank == 0) {
+        unsigned int seed = 42;
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            seeds[i] = seed;
+            for (int j = 0; j < n; ++j) rand_r(&seed);
+        }
+    }
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(seeds.data(), n, MPI_UNSIGNED, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    return seeds;
+}

+    // In row-major tile order, partial last-row tiles form a suffix, with
+    // the partial diagonal last. Three uniform batches avoid padded FLOPs.
+    void gemmBatches(cublasHandle_t handle, int inner, int leading, bool generating,
+                     const double* const* a, const double* const* bPointers,
+                     double* const* c, int count, const std::vector<int>* indices,
+                     double alpha, double beta) {
+        auto tileAt = [&](int i) -> const Tile& { return tiles[indices ? (*indices)[i] : i]; };
+        int full = count, partialDiagonal = 0;
+        const int edge = blocks ? width(blocks - 1) : b;
+        if (edge < b && count) {
+            full = 0;
+            while (full < count && tileAt(full).row != blocks - 1) ++full;
+            partialDiagonal = tileAt(count - 1).column == blocks - 1 ? 1 : 0;
+        }
+        const cublasOperation_t opA = generating ? CUBLAS_OP_T : CUBLAS_OP_N;
+        const cublasOperation_t opB = generating ? CUBLAS_OP_N : CUBLAS_OP_T;
+        auto launch = [&](int rows, int columns, int offset, int number) {
+            if (number)
+                BLAS_CHECK(cublasDgemmBatched(handle, opA, opB, rows, columns, inner, &alpha,
+                    a + offset, leading, bPointers + offset, leading, &beta, c + offset, b, number));
+        };
+        launch(b, b, 0, full);
+        launch(edge, b, full, count - full - partialDiagonal);
+        launch(edge, edge, count - partialDiagonal, partialDiagonal);
+    }
+
+    void launchBatch(const std::vector<int>& indices, int k, double* destination, double alpha, bool bulk) {
+        const int count = mpiCount(indices.size());
         if (!count) return;
+        auto& a = bulk ? bulkLeft : left;
+        auto& bPointers = bulk ? bulkRight : right;
+        auto& c = bulk ? bulkOutput : output;
+        auto* deviceA = bulk ? bulkLeftPointers.data() : leftPointers.data();
+        auto* deviceB = bulk ? bulkRightPointers.data() : rightPointers.data();
+        auto* deviceC = bulk ? bulkOutputPointers.data() : outputPointers.data();
+        const cudaStream_t stream = bulk ? gpu.bulkStream : gpu.stream;
         #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) if(count > 8)
         for (int i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
-            const int index = active[i];
-            left[i] = rowPanel.data() + rowSlot[tiles[index].row] * elements;
-            right[i] = columnPanel.data() + columnSlot[tiles[index].column] * elements;
-            output[i] = destination + static_cast<size_t>(index) * elements;
+            const int index = indices[i];
+            a[i] = grid.size == 1 ? tile(tiles[index].row, k)
+                : rowData(k) + rowSlot[tiles[index].row] * elements;
+            bPointers[i] = grid.size == 1 ? tile(tiles[index].column, k)
+                : columnData(k) + columnSlot[tiles[index].column] * elements;
+            c[i] = destination + static_cast<size_t>(index) * elements;
         }
-        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(leftPointers.data(), left.data(), count * sizeof(double*), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, gpu.stream));
-        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(rightPointers.data(), right.data(), count * sizeof(double*), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, gpu.stream));
-        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(outputPointers.data(), output.data(), count * sizeof(double*), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, gpu.stream));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(deviceA, a.data(), count * sizeof(double*), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(deviceB, bPointers.data(), count * sizeof(double*), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(deviceC, c.data(), count * sizeof(double*), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));
         const double beta = 1.0;
-        BLAS_CHECK(cublasDgemmBatched(gpu.blas, CUBLAS_OP_N, CUBLAS_OP_T, b, b, b, &alpha,
-            leftPointers.data(), b, rightPointers.data(), b, &beta, outputPointers.data(), b, count));
+        gemmBatches(bulk ? gpu.bulkBlas : gpu.blas, width(k), b, false,
+            deviceA, deviceB, deviceC, count, &indices, alpha, beta);
+    }
+
+    void multiplyPanel(int k, int firstBlock, double* destination, double alpha, bool lookahead = false) {
+        if (grid.size == 1) gpu.synchronize();
+        if (lookahead) {
+            // Trailing tiles must have received the previous update before
+            // applying this one. The next panel and MPI exchange have already
+            // run concurrently with that previous update.
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(gpu.bulkDone));
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(gpu.panelsReady, gpu.stream));
+        }
+        active.clear(); bulkActive.clear();
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < tiles.size(); ++i)
+            if (tiles[i].column >= firstBlock) {
+                auto& indices = lookahead && tiles[i].column > firstBlock ? bulkActive : active;
+                indices.push_back(static_cast<int>(i));
+            }
+        launchBatch(active, k, destination, alpha, false);
+        if (lookahead) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamWaitEvent(gpu.bulkStream, gpu.panelsReady, 0));
+            launchBatch(bulkActive, k, destination, alpha, true);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(gpu.bulkDone, gpu.bulkStream));
+        }
     }

 // glibc rand_r advances its 32-bit LCG three times per output. Jumping to
 // each row's initial state preserves the original seed-42 stream exactly,
 // independent of MPI ownership and the OpenMP schedule.
@@ -145,6 +180,24 @@
     return sizeof(unsigned int) == 4;

+std::vector<unsigned int> prepareRowSeeds(int n) {
+    int localCompatible = compatibleRandomGenerator() ? 1 : 0, allCompatible = 0;
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Allreduce(&localCompatible, &allCompatible, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MIN, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    if (allCompatible) return {};
+    // Preserve rand_r semantics on other C libraries too. Only this less
+    // common initialization path needs a serial scan of the RNG states.
+    std::vector<unsigned int> seeds(n);
+    if (worldRank == 0) {
+        unsigned int seed = 42;
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            seeds[i] = seed;
+            for (int j = 0; j < n; ++j) rand_r(&seed);
+        }
+    }
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(seeds.data(), n, MPI_UNSIGNED, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    return seeds;
+}

+    // In row-major tile order, partial last-row tiles form a suffix, with
+    // the partial diagonal last. Three uniform batches avoid padded FLOPs.
+    void gemmBatches(cublasHandle_t handle, int inner, int leading, bool generating,
+                     const double* const* a, const double* const* bPointers,
+                     double* const* c, int count, const std::vector<int>* indices,
+                     double alpha, double beta) {
+        auto tileAt = [&](int i) -> const Tile& { return tiles[indices ? (*indices)[i] : i]; };
+        int full = count, partialDiagonal = 0;
+        const int edge = blocks ? width(blocks - 1) : b;
+        if (edge < b && count) {
+            full = 0;
+            while (full < count && tileAt(full).row != blocks - 1) ++full;
+            partialDiagonal = tileAt(count - 1).column == blocks - 1 ? 1 : 0;
+        }
+        const cublasOperation_t opA = generating ? CUBLAS_OP_T : CUBLAS_OP_N;
+        const cublasOperation_t opB = generating ? CUBLAS_OP_N : CUBLAS_OP_T;
+        auto launch = [&](int rows, int columns, int offset, int number) {
+            if (number)
+                BLAS_CHECK(cublasDgemmBatched(handle, opA, opB, rows, columns, inner, &alpha,
+                    a + offset, leading, bPointers + offset, leading, &beta, c + offset, b, number));
+        };
+        launch(b, b, 0, full);
+        launch(edge, b, full, count - full - partialDiagonal);
+        launch(edge, edge, count - partialDiagonal, partialDiagonal);
+    }
+
+    void launchBatch(const std::vector<int>& indices, int k, double* destination, double alpha, bool bulk) {
+        const int count = mpiCount(indices.size());

+        auto* deviceC = bulk ? bulkOutputPointers.data() : outputPointers.data();

+    void multiplyPanel(int k, int firstBlock, double* destination, double alpha, bool lookahead = false) {
+        if (grid.size == 1) gpu.synchronize();
+        if (lookahead) {
+            // Trailing tiles must have received the previous update before
+            // applying this one. The next panel and MPI exchange have already
+            // run concurrently with that previous update.
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(gpu.bulkDone));
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(gpu.panelsReady, gpu.stream));
+        }
+        active.clear(); bulkActive.clear();
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < tiles.size(); ++i)
+            if (tiles[i].column >= firstBlock) {
+                auto& indices = lookahead && tiles[i].column > firstBlock ? bulkActive : active;
+                indices.push_back(static_cast<int>(i));
+            }
+        launchBatch(active, k, destination, alpha, false);
+        if (lookahead) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamWaitEvent(gpu.bulkStream, gpu.panelsReady, 0));
+            launchBatch(bulkActive, k, destination, alpha, true);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(gpu.bulkDone, gpu.bulkStream));
+        }
     }

Builds and validation passed on 1–4 ranks, including 8193×8193 matrices. CUDA memory and initialization checks reported zero errors and leaks.

 // glibc rand_r advances its 32-bit LCG three times per output. Jumping to
 // each row's initial state preserves the original seed-42 stream exactly,
 // independent of MPI ownership and the OpenMP schedule.
@@ -145,6 +180,24 @@
     return sizeof(unsigned int) == 4;
 }
 
+std::vector<unsigned int> prepareRowSeeds(int n) {
+    int localCompatible = compatibleRandomGenerator() ? 1 : 0, allCompatible = 0;
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Allreduce(&localCompatible, &allCompatible, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MIN, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    if (allCompatible) return {};
+    // Preserve rand_r semantics on other C libraries too. Only this less
+    // common initialization path needs a serial scan of the RNG states.
+    std::vector<unsigned int> seeds(n);
+    if (worldRank == 0) {
+        unsigned int seed = 42;
+        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+            seeds[i] = seed;
+            for (int j = 0; j < n; ++j) rand_r(&seed);
+        }
+    }
+    MPI_CHECK(MPI_Bcast(seeds.data(), n, MPI_UNSIGNED, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD));
+    return seeds;
+}

+    // In row-major tile order, partial last-row tiles form a suffix, with
+    // the partial diagonal last. Three uniform batches avoid padded FLOPs.
+    void gemmBatches(cublasHandle_t handle, int inner, int leading, bool generating,
+                     const double* const* a, const double* const* bPointers,
+                     double* const* c, int count, const std::vector<int>* indices,
+                     double alpha, double beta) {
+        auto tileAt = [&](int i) -> const Tile& { return tiles[indices ? (*indices)[i] : i]; };
+        int full = count, partialDiagonal = 0;
+        const int edge = blocks ? width(blocks - 1) : b;
+        if (edge < b && count) {
+            full = 0;
+            while (full < count && tileAt(full).row != blocks - 1) ++full;
+            partialDiagonal = tileAt(count - 1).column == blocks - 1 ? 1 : 0;
+        }
+        const cublasOperation_t opA = generating ? CUBLAS_OP_T : CUBLAS_OP_N;
+        const cublasOperation_t opB = generating ? CUBLAS_OP_N : CUBLAS_OP_T;
+        auto launch = [&](int rows, int columns, int offset, int number) {
+            if (number)
+                BLAS_CHECK(cublasDgemmBatched(handle, opA, opB, rows, columns, inner, &alpha,
+                    a + offset, leading, bPointers + offset, leading, &beta, c + offset, b, number));
+        };
+        launch(b, b, 0, full);
+        launch(edge, b, full, count - full - partialDiagonal);
+        launch(edge, edge, count - partialDiagonal, partialDiagonal);
+    }
+

+    void multiplyPanel(int k, int firstBlock, double* destination, double alpha, bool lookahead = false) {
+        if (grid.size == 1) gpu.synchronize();
+        if (lookahead) {
+            // Trailing tiles must have received the previous update before
+            // applying this one. The next panel and MPI exchange have already
+            // run concurrently with that previous update.
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(gpu.bulkDone));
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(gpu.panelsReady, gpu.stream));
+        }
+        active.clear(); bulkActive.clear();
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < tiles.size(); ++i)
+            if (tiles[i].column >= firstBlock) {
+                auto& indices = lookahead && tiles[i].column > firstBlock ? bulkActive : active;
+                indices.push_back(static_cast<int>(i));
+            }
+        launchBatch(active, k, destination, alpha, false);
+        if (lookahead) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamWaitEvent(gpu.bulkStream, gpu.panelsReady, 0));
+            launchBatch(bulkActive, k, destination, alpha, true);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(gpu.bulkDone, gpu.bulkStream));
+        }
     }
